/**
 * 가장 가까운 행정구역 중심 찾기
 */
const findNearestRegion = (lat: number, lng: number): string => {
    let nearestRegion = "서울특별시";
    let minDistance = Infinity;

    for (let index = 0; index < REGIONAL_CENTER_ENTRIES.length; index += 1) {
        const [region, center] = REGIONAL_CENTER_ENTRIES[index];
        const distance = getDistance(lat, lng, center.lat, center.lng);
        if (distance < minDistance) {
            minDistance = distance;
            nearestRegion = region;
        }
    }

    return nearestRegion;
};

/**
 * 가장 가까운 서울 자치구 중심 찾기 (주소에 구가 없을 때의 보조 경로)
 */
const findNearestDistrict = (lat: number, lng: number): string | null => {
    let nearestDistrict: string | null = null;
    let minDistance = Infinity;

    for (let index = 0; index < SEOUL_DISTRICT_ENTRIES.length; index += 1) {
        const [district, center] = SEOUL_DISTRICT_ENTRIES[index];
        const distance = getDistance(lat, lng, center.lat, center.lng);
        if (distance < minDistance) {
            minDistance = distance;
            nearestDistrict = district;
        }
    }

    return nearestDistrict;
};

/**
 * 식당 한 건에서 클러스터링에 쓰는 파생 값.
 *
 * 담고 있는 값(id/주소/lat/lng/카테고리)은 계산에 실제로 쓰는 원본 필드이므로,
 * 식당을 제자리에서 수정해도 이전 결과가 재사용되지 않습니다.
 *
 * 행정구역과 자치구는 서로 다른 호출 경로만 쓰므로 각각 필요할 때 계산합니다
 * (getRegionalClusters 는 행정구역만, getSeoulDistrictClusters 는 자치구만 씁니다).
 * 미계산 표시는 undefined 입니다 - 계산 결과 자체가 null 일 수 있기 때문입니다.
 */
type RestaurantClusterEntry = {
    id: string;
    /** road 우선, 없으면 jibun (이전 구현과 동일) */
    address: string;
    lat: Restaurant['lat'];
    lng: Restaurant['lng'];
    /** 이전 구현과 동일한 카테고리 값. 문자열이 아닐 수 있어 Map 키로 그대로 씁니다. */
    categoryLabel: string;
    hasCoordinates: boolean;
    region?: string | null;
    district?: string | null;
};

/** 목록 -> 파생 항목 배열. 같은 목록에 대해 매번 다시 만들지 않습니다. */
const clusterEntriesCache = new WeakMap<readonly Restaurant[], Array<RestaurantClusterEntry | undefined>>();

const clusterAddressOf = (restaurant: Restaurant): string =>
    restaurant.road_address || restaurant.jibun_address || '';

const clusterCategoryLabel = (restaurant: Restaurant): string => {
    const categories = restaurant.categories;
    return (Array.isArray(categories) ? categories[0] : (restaurant.category || '기타')) as string;
};

const createClusterEntry = (restaurant: Restaurant): RestaurantClusterEntry => {
    const lat = restaurant.lat;
    const lng = restaurant.lng;
    return {
        id: restaurant.id,
        address: clusterAddressOf(restaurant),
        lat,
        lng,
        categoryLabel: clusterCategoryLabel(restaurant),
        hasCoordinates: Boolean(lat) && Boolean(lng),
    };
};

const isClusterEntryCurrent = (restaurant: Restaurant, entry: RestaurantClusterEntry): boolean =>
    entry.id === restaurant.id
    && entry.address === clusterAddressOf(restaurant)
    && Object.is(entry.lat, restaurant.lat)
    && Object.is(entry.lng, restaurant.lng)
    && entry.categoryLabel === clusterCategoryLabel(restaurant);

/**
 * 캐시된 항목 배열이 지금 목록과 같은지 확인합니다.
 * 길이와 자리별 원본 필드를 비교하므로 목록 제자리 수정(push/splice)과 식당 필드
 * 제자리 수정을 모두 감지합니다.
 */
const isClusterEntriesCurrent = (
    restaurants: readonly Restaurant[],
    entries: ReadonlyArray<RestaurantClusterEntry | undefined>,
): boolean => {
    if (entries.length !== restaurants.length) return false;

    for (let index = 0; index < entries.length; index += 1) {
        const restaurant = restaurants[index];
        const entry = entries[index];

        if (!restaurant) {
            if (entry) return false;
            continue;
        }

        if (!entry || !isClusterEntryCurrent(restaurant, entry)) return false;
    }

    return true;
};

const buildClusterEntries = (restaurants: readonly Restaurant[]): Array<RestaurantClusterEntry | undefined> => {
    const entries: Array<RestaurantClusterEntry | undefined> = new Array(restaurants.length);

    for (let index = 0; index < restaurants.length; index += 1) {
        const restaurant = restaurants[index];
        if (!restaurant) continue;
        entries[index] = createClusterEntry(restaurant);
    }

    return entries;
};

/** 캐시가 있으면 재사용하고, 없으면 만들어 저장합니다. */
const resolveClusterEntries = (restaurants: readonly Restaurant[]) => {
    const cached = clusterEntriesCache.get(restaurants);
    if (cached && isClusterEntriesCurrent(restaurants, cached)) return cached;

    const built = buildClusterEntries(restaurants);
    clusterEntriesCache.set(restaurants, built);
    return built;
};

/**
 * 캐시가 이미 있을 때만 재사용합니다(없으면 null).
 *
 * getSeoulDistrictClusters 는 이 경로로만 캐시를 씁니다. 캐시가 비어 있는데 항목을
 * 만들어 저장하면, 그 목록으로 이 함수만 호출하는 경우(캐시를 재사용할 기회가 없는
 * 단발 호출)에 순수한 추가 비용이 됩니다. 그래서 없으면 그때그때 계산합니다.
 */
const lookupClusterEntries = (restaurants: readonly Restaurant[]) => {
    const cached = clusterEntriesCache.get(restaurants);
    return cached && isClusterEntriesCurrent(restaurants, cached) ? cached : null;
};

/**
 * 행정구역: 1) 주소에 정식 명칭 포함, 2) 주소 접두어 약어, 3) 좌표상 가장 가까운 중심.
 * 이전 extractRegionFromAddress + findNearestRegion 과 같은 순서/같은 결과입니다.
 */
const computeRegion = (address: string, hasCoordinates: boolean, lat: number, lng: number): string | null => {
    for (let index = 0; index < REGIONAL_CENTER_NAMES.length; index += 1) {
        if (address.includes(REGIONAL_CENTER_NAMES[index])) {
            return REGIONAL_CENTER_NAMES[index];
        }
    }

    for (let index = 0; index < SHORT_REGION_ENTRIES.length; index += 1) {
        const [short, full] = SHORT_REGION_ENTRIES[index];
        if (address.startsWith(short)) {
            return full;
        }
    }

    return hasCoordinates ? findNearestRegion(lat, lng) : null;
};

const resolveClusterRegion = (entry: RestaurantClusterEntry): string | null => {
    if (entry.region !== undefined) return entry.region;

    const region = computeRegion(entry.address, entry.hasCoordinates, Number(entry.lat), Number(entry.lng));
    entry.region = region;
    return region;
};

/**
 * 카테고리 빈도 내림차순 상위 3개.
 * 동률은 Map 삽입 순서(먼저 등장한 순서)를 따릅니다 - 이전 구현과 동일합니다.
 */
const topCategories = (counts: Map<string, number>): string[] =>
    Array.from(counts.entries())
        .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]) // 빈도수 내림차순 정렬
        .slice(0, 3) // 상위 3개만 추출
        .map((entry) => entry[0]);

/**
 * 17개 행정구역 중앙 기준 클러스터링 (줌 레벨 8 이하)
 * 
 * 각 맛집을 가장 가까운 행정구역 중심에 할당하여 17개 클러스터 생성
 * 
 * @param restaurants 레스토랑 목록
 * @returns 행정구역별 클러스터 배열
 */
export const getRegionalClusters = (restaurants: Restaurant[]): RegionalCluster[] => {
    // 각 행정구역별로 맛집 그룹화
    const regionMap = new Map<string, {
        restaurantIds: string[];
        categories: Map<string, number>;
        popularityTier: "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard";
    }>();

    // 모든 17개 행정구역 초기화
    for (let index = 0; index < REGIONAL_CENTER_NAMES.length; index += 1) {
        regionMap.set(REGIONAL_CENTER_NAMES[index], { restaurantIds: [], categories: new Map(), popularityTier: "standard" });
    }

    // 각 맛집을 행정구역에 할당 (주소 우선, 없으면 좌표로 가장 가까운 행정구역)
    const entries = resolveClusterEntries(restaurants);

    for (let index = 0; index < restaurants.length; index += 1) {
        const restaurant = restaurants[index];
        const entry = entries[index];
        if (!restaurant || !entry || !entry.hasCoordinates) continue;

        const region = resolveClusterRegion(entry);
        if (!region) continue;

        const group = regionMap.get(region);
        if (!group) continue;

        group.restaurantIds.push(restaurant.id);
        group.popularityTier = bestPopularityTier(group.popularityTier, restaurant.popularity_tier);

        // 카테고리 추가
        const category = entry.categoryLabel;
        if (category) {
            group.categories.set(category, (group.categories.get(category) || 0) + 1);
        }
    }

    // 맛집이 있는 행정구역만 클러스터로 반환
    const clusters: RegionalCluster[] = [];

    for (const [region, group] of regionMap.entries()) {
        if (group.restaurantIds.length > 0) {
            clusters.push({
                region,
                center: REGIONAL_CENTERS[region],
                count: group.restaurantIds.length,
                restaurantIds: group.restaurantIds,
                categories: topCategories(group.categories),
                popularityTier: group.popularityTier,
            });
        }
    }

    return clusters;
};

/**
 * 서울 자치구 클러스터링 결과 인터페이스
 * clusters: 마커 3개 이상인 구 (클러스터로 표시)
 * individualRestaurants: 마커 2개 이하인 구의 맛집들 (개별 마커로 표시)
 */
export interface SeoulDistrictClusterResult {
    clusters: SeoulDistrictCluster[];
    individualRestaurantIds: string[];
}

/** 구 단위 그룹화 결과. positions 배열 대신 합계를 누적합니다(더하는 순서는 동일). */
type SeoulDistrictGroup = {
    restaurantIds: string[];
    categories: Map<string, number>;
    latSum: number;
    lngSum: number;
    popularityTier: "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard";
};

/**
 * 목록 전체를 25개 구로 한 번 분류한 결과.
 *
 * 지도 갱신 1회는 같은 목록으로 getSeoulDistrictClusters 를 minClusterSize 1과 3으로
 * 두 번 호출합니다. 이전 구현은 구 분류(서울 판정 + 구 이름 25회 검사 + 필요 시 25개
 * 중심 거리 계산)를 두 번 반복했습니다. 분류를 한 번만 하고 minClusterSize 별 결과는
 * 그룹화에서 투영합니다.
 */
type SeoulDistrictGrouping = {
    /** 이 그룹화를 만든 파생 항목 배열. 배열 정체성으로 재사용 여부를 판단합니다. */
    entriesRef: Array<RestaurantClusterEntry | undefined> | null;
    groups: Map<string, SeoulDistrictGroup>;
};

const seoulGroupingCache = new WeakMap<readonly Restaurant[], SeoulDistrictGrouping>();

const createSeoulDistrictGroups = (): Map<string, SeoulDistrictGroup> => {
    const groups = new Map<string, SeoulDistrictGroup>();

    // 25개 구 초기화
    for (let index = 0; index < SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES.length; index += 1) {
        groups.set(SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES[index], {
            restaurantIds: [],
            categories: new Map(),
            latSum: 0,
            lngSum: 0,
            popularityTier: "standard",
        });
    }

    return groups;
};

/**
 * 캐시된 파생 항목이 있는 경우의 구 그룹화.
 *
 * 항목이 이미 구 이름(district)을 계산해 두었으면 그 값을 그대로 씁니다. 항목은
 * 목록과 같은지 검증된 뒤에만 넘어오므로 id/좌표/주소는 원본과 같은 값입니다.
 */
const buildSeoulDistrictGroupingFromEntries = (
    restaurants: readonly Restaurant[],
    entries: Array<RestaurantClusterEntry | undefined>,
): SeoulDistrictGrouping => {
    const groups = createSeoulDistrictGroups();

    for (let index = 0; index < restaurants.length; index += 1) {
        const restaurant = restaurants[index];
        if (!restaurant) continue;

        const entry = entries[index];
        if (!entry) continue;

        const lat = restaurant.lat;
        const lng = restaurant.lng;
        if (!lat || !lng) continue;

        // 구 이름 매칭이 행당 비용을 지배합니다. 4,000건 실측에서 매칭을 작은 함수로
        // 빼면 호출 오버헤드만 약 0.13ms 가 더 붙어(0.54ms vs 0.40ms), 루프 안에
        // 펼쳐 둡니다. 좌표 보정(구 이름이 없을 때만)은 드문 경로라 함수로 남깁니다.
        if (entry.district === undefined) {
            let matched: string | null = null;

            if (entry.hasCoordinates && entry.address.includes('서울')) {
                for (let name = 0; name < SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES.length; name += 1) {
                    if (entry.address.includes(SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES[name])) {
                        matched = SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES[name];
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (!matched) {
                    matched = findNearestDistrict(lat, lng);
                }
            }

            entry.district = matched;
        }

        const district = entry.district;
        if (!district) continue;

        const group = groups.get(district);
        if (!group) continue;

        group.restaurantIds.push(restaurant.id);
        group.latSum += lat;
        group.lngSum += lng;
        group.popularityTier = bestPopularityTier(group.popularityTier, restaurant.popularity_tier);

        if (entry.categoryLabel) {
            group.categories.set(
                entry.categoryLabel,
                (group.categories.get(entry.categoryLabel) || 0) + 1
            );
        }
    }

    return { entriesRef: entries, groups };
};

/**
 * 파생 항목 캐시가 없을 때(이 목록으로 단발 호출)의 구 그룹화.
 *
 * 주소/좌표에서 구를 직접 계산하며, 순서와 결과는 이전 구현과 같습니다. 두 경로를 한
 * 함수로 합치면 인자와 지역 변수가 다형(항목 배열/없음)이 되고 본문이 커져 최적화가
 * 흐려집니다. 4,000건 단발 호출 실측에서 합친 구현은 0.65ms -> 0.61ms(x1.06)로
 * 밀렸고, 함수를 나눈 뒤 0.57ms(x1.12)로 회복했습니다.
 */
const buildSeoulDistrictGroupingFromRestaurants = (
    restaurants: readonly Restaurant[],
): SeoulDistrictGrouping => {
    const groups = createSeoulDistrictGroups();

    for (let index = 0; index < restaurants.length; index += 1) {
        const restaurant = restaurants[index];
        if (!restaurant) continue;

        const lat = restaurant.lat;
        const lng = restaurant.lng;
        if (!lat || !lng) continue;

        const address = restaurant.road_address || restaurant.jibun_address || '';
        if (!address.includes('서울')) continue;

        let district: string | null = null;

        for (let name = 0; name < SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES.length; name += 1) {
            if (address.includes(SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES[name])) {
                district = SEOUL_DISTRICT_NAMES[name];
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!district) {
            district = findNearestDistrict(lat, lng);
        }

        if (!district) continue;

        const group = groups.get(district);
        if (!group) continue;

        group.restaurantIds.push(restaurant.id);
        group.latSum += lat;
        group.lngSum += lng;
        group.popularityTier = bestPopularityTier(group.popularityTier, restaurant.popularity_tier);

        const categories = restaurant.categories;
        const category = (Array.isArray(categories)
            ? categories[0]
            : (restaurant.category || '기타')) as string;
        if (category) {
            group.categories.set(category, (group.categories.get(category) || 0) + 1);
        }
    }

    return { entriesRef: null, groups };
};

const resolveSeoulDistrictGrouping = (restaurants: readonly Restaurant[]): SeoulDistrictGrouping => {
    // 목록 파생 항목이 이미 있으면(같은 갱신에서 getRegionalClusters 가 먼저 만든 경우)
    // 그룹화를 목록 단위로 재사용합니다. 없으면 캐시하지 않고 그때마다 계산합니다.
    const entries = lookupClusterEntries(restaurants);

    if (entries) {
        const cached = seoulGroupingCache.get(restaurants);
        if (cached && cached.entriesRef === entries) return cached;

        const built = buildSeoulDistrictGroupingFromEntries(restaurants, entries);
        seoulGroupingCache.set(restaurants, built);
        return built;
    }

    return buildSeoulDistrictGroupingFromRestaurants(restaurants);
};

/**
 * 구 단위 그룹화에서 minClusterSize 기준 결과를 투영합니다.
 * 그룹 순서는 SEOUL_DISTRICT_CENTERS 키 순서를 그대로 따릅니다.
 */
const projectSeoulDistrictClusters = (
    grouping: SeoulDistrictGrouping,
    minClusterSize: number
): SeoulDistrictClusterResult => {
    const clusters: SeoulDistrictCluster[] = [];
    const individualRestaurantIds: string[] = [];

    // 캐시에 들어간 그룹화(entriesRef 가 있는 경우)는 다음 호출이 다시 읽습니다. 그때는
    // 호출자가 결과 배열을 수정해도 캐시가 오염되지 않도록 복사해서 넘깁니다. 캐시하지
    // 않은 그룹화는 이 호출이 소유하므로 복사 없이 그대로 넘깁니다.
    const shared = grouping.entriesRef !== null;

    for (const [district, group] of grouping.groups.entries()) {
        const count = group.restaurantIds.length;

        if (count >= minClusterSize) {
            // minClusterSize 이상: 실제 마커들의 중심점 계산
            clusters.push({
                region: district,
                center: { lat: group.latSum / count, lng: group.lngSum / count },
                count,
                restaurantIds: shared ? group.restaurantIds.slice() : group.restaurantIds,
                categories: topCategories(group.categories),
                popularityTier: group.popularityTier,
            });
        } else if (count > 0) {
            // minClusterSize 미만: 개별 마커로 표시
            individualRestaurantIds.push(...group.restaurantIds);
        }
    }

    return { clusters, individualRestaurantIds };
};

/**
 * 서울시 25개 자치구 기준 클러스터링
 * 
 * @param restaurants 레스토랑 목록
 * @param minClusterSize 클러스터로 표시할 최소 마커 수 (기본값: 1)
 *   - minClusterSize=1: 마커가 1개 이상이면 모두 클러스터로 표시 (줌 9-10)
 *   - minClusterSize=3: 마커가 3개 이상일 때만 클러스터, 2개 이하는 개별 마커 (줌 11-12)
 */
export const getSeoulDistrictClusters = (
    restaurants: Restaurant[],
    minClusterSize: number = 1
): SeoulDistrictClusterResult =>
    projectSeoulDistrictClusters(resolveSeoulDistrictGrouping(restaurants), minClusterSize);

    lat: number;
    lng: number;
    accuracy: number | null;
    heading: number | null;
    mode: DeviceLocationMode;
    focusRequestId: number;
    updatedAt: number;
}

    isHeadingMode: boolean;
    isPending: boolean;
}

    location: Pick<DeviceMapLocation, 'accuracy' | 'focusRequestId'> | null;
    maxAccuracyRadius?: number;
    minAccuracyRadius?: number;

    shouldUpdate: boolean;
}

const DEFAULT_HEADING_UPDATE_THRESHOLD_DEGREES = 3;
const DEFAULT_POSITION_UPDATE_THRESHOLD_METERS = 3;
const DEFAULT_ACCURACY_UPDATE_THRESHOLD_METERS = 5;
const EARTH_RADIUS_METERS = 6_371_000;

export function normalizeCompassHeading(value: number | null | undefined): number | null {
    if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) return null;
    return ((value % 360) + 360) % 360;
}

export function resolveDeviceOrientationHeading(
    event: Pick<DeviceOrientationEvent, 'alpha' | 'absolute'> & { webkitCompassHeading?: number | null }
): number | null {
    const webkitHeading = normalizeCompassHeading(event.webkitCompassHeading);
    if (webkitHeading !== null) return webkitHeading;

    if (event.absolute !== true) return null;
    const alphaHeading = normalizeCompassHeading(event.alpha);
    if (alphaHeading === null) return null;

    // DeviceOrientation alpha grows counter-clockwise on many Android browsers;
    // convert it to the same clockwise-from-north convention as compass headings.
    return normalizeCompassHeading(360 - alphaHeading);
}

export function resolveGeolocationHeading(value: number | null | undefined): number | null {
    return normalizeCompassHeading(value);
}

export function resolveDeviceLocationButtonLabel({
    hasLocation,
    isHeadingMode,
    isPending,
}: DeviceLocationButtonStateInput): string {
    if (isPending) return '현재 위치 확인 중';
    if (isHeadingMode) return '현재 위치와 방향 다시 확인';
    if (hasLocation) return '현재 위치 기준 방향 확인';
    return '현재 위치 보기';
}

export function shouldFocusDeviceLocation(
    previousFocusRequestId: number | null,
    location: Pick<DeviceMapLocation, 'focusRequestId'> | null
): boolean {
    return location !== null && previousFocusRequestId !== location.focusRequestId;
}

export function resolveDeviceLocationAccuracyRadius(
    accuracy: number | null | undefined,
    minRadius = 12,
    maxRadius = 500
): number | null {
    if (typeof accuracy !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(accuracy)) return null;
    return Math.max(minRadius, Math.min(accuracy, maxRadius));
}

export function resolveDeviceLocationFocusZoom(currentZoom: number, minimumFocusZoom = 15): number {
    if (typeof currentZoom !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(currentZoom)) return minimumFocusZoom;
    return Math.max(currentZoom, minimumFocusZoom);
}

function toRadians(value: number): number {
    return value * Math.PI / 180;
}

export function getDeviceLocationDistanceMeters(
    first: Pick<DeviceMapLocation, 'lat' | 'lng'>,
    second: Pick<DeviceMapLocation, 'lat' | 'lng'>,
): number {
    const firstLat = toRadians(first.lat);
    const secondLat = toRadians(second.lat);
    const deltaLat = toRadians(second.lat - first.lat);
    const deltaLng = toRadians(second.lng - first.lng);
    const haversine =
        Math.sin(deltaLat / 2) ** 2 +
        Math.cos(firstLat) * Math.cos(secondLat) * Math.sin(deltaLng / 2) ** 2;

    return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_METERS * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(haversine), Math.sqrt(1 - haversine));
}

export function getCompassHeadingDeltaDegrees(
    previousHeading: number | null | undefined,
    nextHeading: number | null | undefined,
): number {
    const previous = normalizeCompassHeading(previousHeading);
    const next = normalizeCompassHeading(nextHeading);
    if (previous === null || next === null) return previous === next ? 0 : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

    const directDelta = Math.abs(next - previous);
    return Math.min(directDelta, 360 - directDelta);
}

function getAccuracyDeltaMeters(
    previousAccuracy: number | null | undefined,
    nextAccuracy: number | null | undefined,
): number {
    if (
        typeof previousAccuracy !== 'number' ||
        !Number.isFinite(previousAccuracy) ||
        typeof nextAccuracy !== 'number' ||
        !Number.isFinite(nextAccuracy)
    ) {
        return previousAccuracy === nextAccuracy ? 0 : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    }

    return Math.abs(nextAccuracy - previousAccuracy);
}

export function resolveDeviceLocationStateUpdatePlan({
    accuracyThresholdMeters = DEFAULT_ACCURACY_UPDATE_THRESHOLD_METERS,
    headingThresholdDegrees = DEFAULT_HEADING_UPDATE_THRESHOLD_DEGREES,
    next,
    positionThresholdMeters = DEFAULT_POSITION_UPDATE_THRESHOLD_METERS,
    previous,
}: DeviceLocationStateUpdateInput): DeviceLocationStateUpdatePlan {
    if (!previous) {
        return { nextLocation: next, shouldUpdate: true };
    }

    if (previous.focusRequestId !== next.focusRequestId || previous.mode !== next.mode) {
        return { nextLocation: next, shouldUpdate: true };
    }

    const distanceMeters = getDeviceLocationDistanceMeters(previous, next);
    if (distanceMeters >= positionThresholdMeters) {
        return { nextLocation: next, shouldUpdate: true };
    }

    const headingDelta = getCompassHeadingDeltaDegrees(previous.heading, next.heading);
    if (headingDelta >= headingThresholdDegrees) {
        return { nextLocation: next, shouldUpdate: true };
    }

    const accuracyDelta = getAccuracyDeltaMeters(previous.accuracy, next.accuracy);
    if (accuracyDelta >= accuracyThresholdMeters) {
        return { nextLocation: next, shouldUpdate: true };
    }

    return { nextLocation: previous, shouldUpdate: false };
}

export function resolveDeviceLocationMapRenderPlan({
    currentZoom,
    location,
    maxAccuracyRadius = 500,
    minAccuracyRadius = 12,
    minimumFocusZoom = 15,
    previousFocusRequestId,
}: DeviceLocationMapRenderPlanInput): DeviceLocationMapRenderPlan {
    if (!location) {
        return {
            accuracyRadius: null,
            focusZoom: resolveDeviceLocationFocusZoom(currentZoom, minimumFocusZoom),
            hasLocation: false,
            nextFocusedRequestId: null,
            shouldFocus: false,
        };
    }

    const shouldFocus = shouldFocusDeviceLocation(previousFocusRequestId, location);

    return {
        accuracyRadius: resolveDeviceLocationAccuracyRadius(location.accuracy, minAccuracyRadius, maxAccuracyRadius),
        focusZoom: resolveDeviceLocationFocusZoom(currentZoom, minimumFocusZoom),
        hasLocation: true,
        nextFocusedRequestId: shouldFocus ? location.focusRequestId : previousFocusRequestId,
        shouldFocus,
    };
}

export function buildDeviceLocationMarkerHtml(location: Pick<DeviceMapLocation, 'accuracy' | 'heading' | 'mode'>): string {
    const heading = normalizeCompassHeading(location.heading);
    const showHeading = location.mode === 'heading' && heading !== null;
    const markerHeading = heading === null ? null : Math.round(heading * 10) / 10;
    const accuracyRadius = resolveDeviceLocationAccuracyRadius(location.accuracy);
    const accuracyLabel = accuracyRadius === null
        ? '현재 위치'
        : `${Math.round(accuracyRadius)}m`;

    return `
        <div style="position:relative;width:56px;height:56px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;pointer-events:none;">
            <div style="position:absolute;width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9999px;background:rgba(37,99,235,0.14);border:1px solid rgba(37,99,235,0.25);"></div>
            ${showHeading ? `<div aria-hidden="true" style="position:absolute;left:50%;top:2px;width:0;height:0;border-left:7px solid transparent;border-right:7px solid transparent;border-bottom:22px solid rgba(37,99,235,0.68);transform:translateX(-50%) rotate(${markerHeading}deg);transform-origin:50% 26px;filter:drop-shadow(0 2px 3px rgba(15,23,42,0.22));"></div>` : ''}
            <div style="position:relative;width:18px;height:18px;border-radius:9999px;background:#2563eb;border:3px solid white;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(37,99,235,0.45),0 0 0 1px rgba(15,23,42,0.16);"></div>
            <span style="position:absolute;left:50%;top:44px;transform:translateX(-50%);white-space:nowrap;border-radius:9999px;background:rgba(15,23,42,0.78);color:white;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;line-height:1;padding:4px 7px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(15,23,42,0.18);">${showHeading ? `${Math.round(heading)}°` : accuracyLabel}</span>
        </div>
    `;
}


===== apps/web/lib/device-performance.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 5146 | sha256 bcb8a6f5dd29ecfd33846452c7531777579b448006fccbbf6c47c5dcf7af1b92 =====
/**
 * 디바이스 성능 감지 유틸리티
 * 
 * 디바이스 하드웨어 사양을 감지하여 성능 등급을 반환합니다.
 * 클러스터링 최적화, 렌더링 품질 조정 등에 활용됩니다.
 */
import { debugLog } from '@/lib/debug-log';

/**
 * 디바이스 성능 등급
 */
export type DevicePerformanceLevel = 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH';

/**
 * 성능 등급별 클러스터 옵션
 */
export interface PerformanceClusterOptions {
    maxZoom: number;
    radius: number;
    minPoints: number;
}

/**
 * Navigator 인터페이스 확장 (deviceMemory, hardwareConcurrency)
 */
interface NavigatorWithMemory extends Navigator {
    deviceMemory?: number;
}

/**
 * 저사양 디바이스 User-Agent 패턴
 * Chrome DevTools 기본 디바이스 목록 기반
 */
const LOW_END_DEVICE_PATTERNS = [
    // BlackBerry
    /BlackBerry\s?Z30/i,
    /PlayBook/i,
    // Samsung 구형
    /SM-N900|GT-N7100|SM-G730|GT-I9300|SM-G900/i, // Galaxy Note 3, S III 등
    // Nexus
    /Nexus\s?(4|5|6|7|10)/i,
    // Nokia
    /Lumia\s?(520|630|930|950)/i,
    /Nokia\s?N9/i,
    // LG
    /LG-D160|LG-D320|Optimus/i,
    // Motorola
    /Moto\s?G|XT1068|XT1032/i,
    // Kindle
    /Kindle|KFTT|KFOT/i,
];

/**
 * User-Agent에서 저사양 디바이스 감지
 */
const isLowEndDeviceByUA = (): boolean => {
    if (typeof navigator === 'undefined') return false;

    const ua = navigator.userAgent;
    return LOW_END_DEVICE_PATTERNS.some(pattern => pattern.test(ua));
};

/**
 * 디바이스 성능 등급 감지
 * 
 * 우선순위:
 * 1. User-Agent 기반 저사양 디바이스 감지
 * 2. navigator.deviceMemory (RAM)
 * 3. navigator.hardwareConcurrency (CPU 코어)
 * 4. 화면 크기 기반 추정
 * 
 * @returns 성능 등급 (LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH)
 */
export const getDevicePerformanceLevel = (): DevicePerformanceLevel => {
    // SSR 환경 처리
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof navigator === 'undefined') {
        return 'MEDIUM';
    }

    // 1. UA 기반 저사양 디바이스 감지
    if (isLowEndDeviceByUA()) {
        if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
            debugLog('[DevicePerformance] LOW - User-Agent 패턴 매칭');
        }
        return 'LOW';
    }

    const nav = navigator as NavigatorWithMemory;

    // 2. deviceMemory API (GB 단위)
    const memory = nav.deviceMemory;

    // 3. hardwareConcurrency (CPU 코어 수)
    const cores = nav.hardwareConcurrency;

    // 4. 성능 등급 결정
    let level: DevicePerformanceLevel;

    if (memory !== undefined) {
        if (memory <= 2) {
            level = 'LOW';
        } else if (memory <= 4) {
            level = 'MEDIUM';
        } else {
            level = 'HIGH';
        }
    } else if (cores !== undefined) {
        if (cores <= 2) {
            level = 'LOW';
        } else if (cores <= 4) {
            level = 'MEDIUM';
        } else {
            level = 'HIGH';
        }
    } else {
        // API 미지원 시 화면 크기로 추정
        const screenWidth = window.screen.width;
        const pixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        const effectiveWidth = screenWidth * pixelRatio;

        if (effectiveWidth <= 720) {
            level = 'LOW';
        } else if (effectiveWidth <= 1440) {
            level = 'MEDIUM';
        } else {
            level = 'HIGH';
        }
    }

    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
        debugLog(`[DevicePerformance] ${level} - Memory: ${memory ?? 'N/A'}GB, Cores: ${cores ?? 'N/A'}`);
    }

    return level;
};

/**
 * 성능 등급별 클러스터 옵션 반환
 * 
 * @param level 성능 등급 (undefined 시 자동 감지)
 * @returns 클러스터 옵션
 */
export const getPerformanceBasedClusterOptions = (
    level?: DevicePerformanceLevel
): PerformanceClusterOptions => {
    const performanceLevel = level ?? getDevicePerformanceLevel();

    switch (performanceLevel) {
        case 'LOW':
            return {
                maxZoom: 14,   // 줌 15부터 개별 마커
                radius: 60,   // 더 넓은 클러스터링
                minPoints: 3, // 최소 3개부터 클러스터
            };
        case 'MEDIUM':
            return {
                maxZoom: 13,  // 줌 14부터 개별 마커
                radius: 50,   // 균형
                minPoints: 2,
            };
        case 'HIGH':
        default:
            return {
                maxZoom: 12,  // 줌 13부터 개별 마커
                radius: 40,   // 정밀한 클러스터링
                minPoints: 2,
            };
    }
};

/**
 * 캐시된 성능 등급 (런타임 중 변하지 않음)
 */
let cachedPerformanceLevel: DevicePerformanceLevel | null = null;

/**
 * 캐시된 성능 등급 가져오기 (싱글톤)
 */
export const getCachedPerformanceLevel = (): DevicePerformanceLevel => {
    if (cachedPerformanceLevel === null) {
        cachedPerformanceLevel = getDevicePerformanceLevel();
    }
    return cachedPerformanceLevel;
};

/**
 * 캐시 초기화 (테스트용)
 */
export const resetPerformanceCache = (): void => {
    cachedPerformanceLevel = null;
};


===== apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/badge-device-capability.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 929 | sha256 d3d0f575f0e483dfbb5ab465379f0f65c95d28ec97b0190335710cd840125b68 =====
import { getTierFromHardware, type DevicePerformance } from "@/hooks/useDevicePerformance";

/** Conservative policy for the optional viewer, not a physical device/model detector. */
export function shouldUseStaticBadgeViewer(
  performance: Pick<DevicePerformance, "isLowEndDevice" | "cpuCores" | "memoryGB">,
  browser: { userAgent: string; maxTouchPoints: number } | null,
): boolean {
  if (performance.isLowEndDevice || !browser) return true;
  const mobileOrTouch = /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile/i.test(browser.userAgent)
    || browser.maxTouchPoints > 0;
  if (!mobileOrTouch) return false;

  // OS versions cannot identify iPhone hardware. Missing/coarse memory data is
  // deliberately insufficient to opt a touch/mobile device into experimental 3D.
  const { memoryGB, cpuCores } = performance;
  return memoryGB === null || !Number.isFinite(memoryGB)
    || getTierFromHardware(cpuCores, memoryGB) !== "HIGH";
}


===== apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/badge-viewer-lifecycle.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 3306 | sha256 b470fe9fb3c3a8c9984cc075f3be1004f394c6732872b6c6b94e67310372dfd0 =====
import { badgeViewerClosesOnKey } from "./badge-viewer-session";

type BadgeCanvasSurface = {
  style: {
    display: string;
    height: string;
    width: string;
  };
  remove(): void;
  removeEventListener(type: "webglcontextlost", listener: EventListener): void;
};

type BadgeRendererSurface = {
  setPixelRatio(value: number): void;
  setSize(width: number, height: number, updateStyle?: boolean): void;
};

export function sizeBadgeCanvas(
  canvas: BadgeCanvasSurface,
  renderer: Pick<BadgeRendererSurface, "setPixelRatio" | "setSize">,
  devicePixelRatio: number,
  logicalSize = 120,
): void {
  canvas.style.display = "block";
  canvas.style.width = `${logicalSize}px`;
  canvas.style.height = `${logicalSize}px`;
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(Math.max(devicePixelRatio || 1, 1), 2));
  renderer.setSize(logicalSize, logicalSize, false);
}

export function startBadgeRenderLoop({
  reducedMotion,
  rotate,
  render,
  requestFrame,
  cancelFrame,
}: {
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  rotate: () => void;
  render: () => void;
  requestFrame: (callback: FrameRequestCallback) => number;
  cancelFrame: (handle: number) => void;
}): () => void {
  let stopped = false;
  let frame: number | null = null;

  const draw: FrameRequestCallback = () => {
    if (stopped) return;
    if (!reducedMotion) rotate();
    render();
    if (!reducedMotion) frame = requestFrame(draw);
  };

  draw(0);

  return () => {
    stopped = true;
    if (frame !== null) cancelFrame(frame);
    frame = null;
  };
}

export function releaseBadgeRendererResources({
  canvas,
  contextLossListener,
  stopAnimation,
  disposeGeometry,
  disposeMaterial,
  disposeRenderer,
  forceContextLoss,
}: {
  canvas: BadgeCanvasSurface | null;
  contextLossListener: EventListener | null;
  stopAnimation: (() => void) | null;
  disposeGeometry: () => void;
  disposeMaterial: () => void;
  disposeRenderer: () => void;
  forceContextLoss: () => void;
}): void {
  stopAnimation?.();
  if (canvas && contextLossListener) {
    canvas.removeEventListener("webglcontextlost", contextLossListener);
  }
  disposeGeometry();
  disposeMaterial();
  disposeRenderer();
  forceContextLoss();
  canvas?.remove();
}

export function createBadgeContextLossHandler({
  releaseLostContext,
  tryRecover,
  recover,
  fallback,
}: {
  releaseLostContext: () => void;
  tryRecover: () => boolean;
  recover: () => void;
  fallback: () => void;
}): EventListener {
  return (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    releaseLostContext();
    if (tryRecover()) {
      recover();
      return;
    }
    fallback();
  };
}

export function createBadgeContextRecoveryLimit(maxRecoveries = 1): () => boolean {
  let recoveryCount = 0;
  return () => {
    if (recoveryCount >= maxRecoveries) return false;
    recoveryCount += 1;
    return true;
  };
}

export function handleBadgeViewerCloseKey(
  event: Pick<KeyboardEvent, "key" | "preventDefault" | "stopPropagation">,
  onClose: () => void,
): boolean {
  if (!badgeViewerClosesOnKey(event.key)) return false;
  event.preventDefault();
  event.stopPropagation();
  onClose();
  return true;
}

export function restoreBadgeViewerFocus(
  opener: Pick<HTMLElement, "focus"> | null,
): boolean {
  if (!opener) return false;
  opener.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  return true;
}

export type EvidenceContractCode = (typeof EvidenceContractCodes)[keyof typeof EvidenceContractCodes];

export class EvidenceContractError extends Error {
  readonly code: EvidenceContractCode;

  constructor(code: EvidenceContractCode) {
    super(code);
    this.name = "EvidenceContractError";
    this.code = code;
  }
}


===== apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/frames.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 9213 | sha256 253fda0e407c5c9b0353984ff639d4dc6688b2f4df9c5c90bcdc764d22cf90c1 =====
import { buildCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl } from "../youtube-url";
import {
  FRAME_SAMPLING,
  LEVEL1_FRAME_TYPES,
} from "./constants";
import { formatStorageObjectKey } from "./storage-key.mjs";
import { EvidenceContractCodes, EvidenceContractError } from "./errors";
import {
  assertNoForbiddenPayloadKeys,
  isConfidenceLevel,
  isEvidenceFrameType,
  isEvidenceLevel,
  isModerationStatus,
  parseMember,
  requireContentSha256,
  requireDetectedName,
  requireFrameId,
  requireImageUrl,
  requireTimestampMs,
  requireYouTubeVideoId,
} from "./guards";
import type {
  EvidenceFrameDraft,
  FrameSamplingPlan,
  RestaurantEvidenceFrame,
} from "./types";

const FRAME_PAYLOAD_KEYS = [
  "frame_id",
  "content_sha256",
  "video_id",
  "timestamp_ms",
  "storage_object_key",
  "youtube_seek_url",
  "frame_type",
  "evidence_level",
  "confidence_level",
  "moderation_status",
  "detected_shop_name",
  "detected_branch_name",
  "image_url",
] as const;

export function buildEvidenceSeekUrl(videoId: string, timestampMs: number): string {
  const id = requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId);
  const timestamp = requireTimestampMs(timestampMs);
  const watchUrl = buildCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl(id);
  if (!watchUrl) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidVideoId);
  }
  return `${watchUrl}&t=${Math.floor(timestamp / 1000)}`;
}

export function buildStorageObjectKey(videoId: string, timestampMs: number, contentSha256: string): string {
  const id = requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId);
  const timestamp = requireTimestampMs(timestampMs);
  const hash = requireContentSha256(contentSha256);
  return formatStorageObjectKey(id, timestamp, hash);
}

export function planFrameSamplingWindow(videoId: string, targetTimestampMs: number): FrameSamplingPlan {
  const id = requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId);
  const target = requireTimestampMs(targetTimestampMs);
  return {
    video_id: id,
    target_timestamp_ms: target,
    window_start_ms: Math.max(0, target - FRAME_SAMPLING.windowMs),
    window_end_ms: target + FRAME_SAMPLING.windowMs,
    sample_fps: FRAME_SAMPLING.sampleFpsAfterDecode,
    keyframe_role: FRAME_SAMPLING.keyframeRole,
    loads_full_video_in_node: false,
  };
}

export function isLevel1SourceObserved(
  frame: Pick<RestaurantEvidenceFrame, "evidence_level" | "frame_type" | "moderation_status">,
): boolean {
  return (
    frame.moderation_status === "approved" &&
    frame.evidence_level === "SOURCE_OBSERVED" &&
    (LEVEL1_FRAME_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(frame.frame_type)
  );
}

export function buildEvidenceFrame(draft: EvidenceFrameDraft): RestaurantEvidenceFrame {
  const videoId = requireYouTubeVideoId(draft.video_id);
  const timestampMs = requireTimestampMs(draft.timestamp_ms);
  const contentSha256 = requireContentSha256(draft.content_sha256);
  const frame: RestaurantEvidenceFrame = {
    frame_id: requireFrameId(draft.frame_id),
    content_sha256: contentSha256,
    video_id: videoId,
    timestamp_ms: timestampMs,
    storage_object_key: buildStorageObjectKey(videoId, timestampMs, contentSha256),
    youtube_seek_url: buildEvidenceSeekUrl(videoId, timestampMs),
    frame_type: parseMember(draft.frame_type, isEvidenceFrameType, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrameType),
    evidence_level: parseMember(draft.evidence_level, isEvidenceLevel, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidEvidenceLevel),
    confidence_level: parseMember(draft.confidence_level, isConfidenceLevel, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidConfidence),
    moderation_status: parseMember(draft.moderation_status, isModerationStatus, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidModeration),
    detected_shop_name: requireDetectedName(draft.detected_shop_name),
    detected_branch_name: requireDetectedName(draft.detected_branch_name),
    image_url: requireImageUrl(draft.image_url),
  };
  return frame;
}

export function parseEvidenceFrame(input: unknown): RestaurantEvidenceFrame {
  if (input === null || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrame);
  }
  assertNoForbiddenPayloadKeys(input);
  const record = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  for (const key of Object.keys(record)) {
    if (!(FRAME_PAYLOAD_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrame);
    }
  }
  const frame = buildEvidenceFrame({
    frame_id: readString(record.frame_id, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrameId),
    content_sha256: readString(record.content_sha256, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidContentSha256),
    video_id: readString(record.video_id, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidVideoId),
    timestamp_ms: readTimestamp(record.timestamp_ms),
    frame_type: record.frame_type as EvidenceFrameDraft["frame_type"],
    evidence_level: record.evidence_level as EvidenceFrameDraft["evidence_level"],
    confidence_level: record.confidence_level as EvidenceFrameDraft["confidence_level"],
    moderation_status: record.moderation_status as EvidenceFrameDraft["moderation_status"],
    detected_shop_name: readNullableString(record.detected_shop_name, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName),
    detected_branch_name: readNullableString(record.detected_branch_name, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName),
    image_url: readNullableString(record.image_url, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl),
  });
  if (typeof record.storage_object_key === "string" && record.storage_object_key !== frame.storage_object_key) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.storageKeyMismatch);
  }
  if (typeof record.youtube_seek_url === "string" && record.youtube_seek_url !== frame.youtube_seek_url) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.seekIntegrityMismatch);
  }
  if ("storage_object_key" in record && typeof record.storage_object_key !== "string") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.storageKeyMismatch);
  }
  if ("youtube_seek_url" in record && typeof record.youtube_seek_url !== "string") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.seekIntegrityMismatch);
  }
  return frame;
}

function readString(value: unknown, code: EvidenceContractError["code"]): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  return value;
}

function readNullableString(value: unknown, code: EvidenceContractError["code"]): string | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string") throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  return value;
}

function readTimestamp(value: unknown): number {
  if (typeof value !== "number") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidTimestampMs);
  }
  return value;
}

function sameIdentity(left: RestaurantEvidenceFrame, right: RestaurantEvidenceFrame): boolean {
  return (
    left.content_sha256 === right.content_sha256 &&
    left.video_id === right.video_id &&
    left.timestamp_ms === right.timestamp_ms &&
    left.storage_object_key === right.storage_object_key &&
    left.youtube_seek_url === right.youtube_seek_url &&
    left.frame_type === right.frame_type &&
    left.evidence_level === right.evidence_level &&
    left.confidence_level === right.confidence_level &&
    left.detected_shop_name === right.detected_shop_name &&
    left.detected_branch_name === right.detected_branch_name
  );
}

export function unionApprovedEvidenceFrames(groups: readonly RestaurantEvidenceFrame[][]): RestaurantEvidenceFrame[] {
  const grouped = new Map<string, RestaurantEvidenceFrame[]>();
  for (const group of groups) {
    for (const frame of group) {
      const validated = parseEvidenceFrame(frame);
      const existing = grouped.get(validated.frame_id);
      if (existing) existing.push(validated);
      else grouped.set(validated.frame_id, [validated]);
    }
  }

  const approved: RestaurantEvidenceFrame[] = [];
  for (const copies of grouped.values()) {
    const anchor = copies[0];
    if (copies.some((copy) => !sameIdentity(anchor, copy))) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.duplicateFrameConflict);
    }
    const hasApproved = copies.some((copy) => copy.moderation_status === "approved");
    const hasRejected = copies.some((copy) => copy.moderation_status === "rejected");
    if (hasApproved && hasRejected) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.moderationConflict);
    }
    if (!hasApproved) continue;
    const approvedCopies = copies.filter((copy) => copy.moderation_status === "approved");
    const imageUrls = new Set(approvedCopies.map((copy) => copy.image_url));
    const imageUrl = imageUrls.size === 1 ? approvedCopies[0].image_url : null;
    approved.push(buildEvidenceFrame({ ...approvedCopies[0], image_url: imageUrl, moderation_status: "approved" }));
  }

  approved.sort((left, right) => (
    left.timestamp_ms - right.timestamp_ms || left.frame_id.localeCompare(right.frame_id)
  ));
  const storageKeys = new Set<string>();
  for (const frame of approved) {
    if (storageKeys.has(frame.storage_object_key)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.duplicateFrameConflict);
    }
    storageKeys.add(frame.storage_object_key);
  }
  return approved;
}

import { extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId } from "../youtube-url";
import {
  CALENDAR_DATE_PATTERN,
  CANONICAL_KEY_PATTERN,
  CONFIDENCE_LEVELS,
  CONTENT_SHA256_PATTERN,
  DETECTED_NAME_MAX_LENGTH,
  EVIDENCE_FRAME_TYPES,
  EVIDENCE_LEVELS,
  FORBIDDEN_PAYLOAD_KEYS,
  FRAME_ID_PATTERN,
  ISO_TIMESTAMP_PATTERN,
  MODERATION_STATUSES,
  POPULARITY_TIERS,
  VERSION_TOKEN_PATTERN,
} from "./constants";
import { EvidenceContractCodes, EvidenceContractError } from "./errors";
import type {
  ConfidenceLevel,
  EvidenceFrameType,
  EvidenceLevel,
  ModerationStatus,
  PopularityTier,
} from "./types";

const PHONE_LIKE_PATTERN = /\d{2,4}-\d{3,4}-\d{4}/;

export function requireYouTubeVideoId(value: string): string {
  const videoId = extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(value);
  if (!videoId || videoId !== value) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidVideoId);
  }
  return videoId;
}

export function requireTimestampMs(value: number): number {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidTimestampMs);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireContentSha256(value: string): string {
  if (!CONTENT_SHA256_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidContentSha256);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireFrameId(value: string): string {
  if (!FRAME_ID_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrameId);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireVersionToken(value: string, code: "invalid_scoring_version" | "invalid_cohort_version"): string {
  if (!VERSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireIsoTimestamp(value: string): string {
  if (!ISO_TIMESTAMP_PATTERN.test(value) || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value))) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidComputedAt);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireCalendarDate(value: string): string {
  if (!CALENDAR_DATE_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCalendarDate);
  }
  const [yearText, monthText, dayText] = value.split("-");
  const year = Number(yearText);
  const month = Number(monthText);
  const day = Number(dayText);
  const utc = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  if (
    utc.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
    utc.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
    utc.getUTCDate() !== day
  ) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCalendarDate);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireCanonicalKey(value: string): string {
  if (!CANONICAL_KEY_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCanonicalKey);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireBoundedText(value: string, maxLength: number, code: "invalid_detected_name" | "invalid_closure_record"): string {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (
    trimmed.length === 0 ||
    trimmed.length > maxLength ||
    /[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/.test(trimmed) ||
    trimmed.includes("@") ||
    PHONE_LIKE_PATTERN.test(trimmed)
  ) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  }
  return trimmed;
}

export function requireDetectedName(value: string | null): string | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName);
  }
  if (value.trim() === "") return null;
  return requireBoundedText(value, DETECTED_NAME_MAX_LENGTH, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName);
}

export function requireImageUrl(value: string | null): string | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0 || value.length > 2048) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl);
  }
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(value);
  } catch {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl);
  }
  if (url.protocol !== "https:" || url.username || url.password) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl);
  }
  return value;
}

export function assertNoForbiddenPayloadKeys(value: object): void {
  for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
    if ((FORBIDDEN_PAYLOAD_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.personalDataForbidden);
    }
  }
}

export function isEvidenceLevel(value: unknown): value is EvidenceLevel {
  return typeof value === "string" && (EVIDENCE_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isEvidenceFrameType(value: unknown): value is EvidenceFrameType {
  return typeof value === "string" && (EVIDENCE_FRAME_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isConfidenceLevel(value: unknown): value is ConfidenceLevel {
  return typeof value === "string" && (CONFIDENCE_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isModerationStatus(value: unknown): value is ModerationStatus {
  return typeof value === "string" && (MODERATION_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isPopularityTier(value: unknown): value is PopularityTier {
  return typeof value === "string" && (POPULARITY_TIERS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function parseMember<T extends string>(
  value: unknown,
  guard: (value: unknown) => value is T,
  code: EvidenceContractError["code"],
): T {
  if (!guard(value)) throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  return value;
}

/**
 * Parses a count the way the synthesis `safe_count` does.
 * Null means missing. Zero means a real zero. Booleans are missing.
 * Integral JavaScript numbers are accepted. Decimal strings are missing.
 * Underscores follow Python `int` digit rules. Values above the maximum clamp.
 */
export function safeCount(value: unknown, maximum: number): number | null {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maximum) || maximum < 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidMaximum);
  }
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  if (typeof value === "boolean") return null;
  if (typeof value === "number") {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) return null;
    return Math.min(value, maximum);
  }
  if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  const parsed = parsePythonInteger(value);
  if (parsed === null || parsed < 0) return null;
  return Math.min(parsed, maximum);
}

function parsePythonInteger(value: string): number | null {
  const stripped = value.replaceAll(",", "").trim();
  if (!/^[+-]?\d(?:_?\d)*$/.test(stripped)) return null;
  const digits = stripped.replaceAll("_", "");
  const negative = digits.startsWith("-");
  const magnitude = negative ? digits.slice(1) : digits.replace(/^\+/, "");
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(magnitude)) return null;
  const numeric = Number(magnitude);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(numeric)) return null;
  return negative ? -numeric : numeric;
}

const POPULARITY_TIER_RANK = { standard: 0, rainbow: 1, ember: 2, fire: 3 } as const;

export function publicPopularityTier(value: unknown): "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" {
  return value === "fire" || value === "ember" || value === "rainbow" ? value : "standard";
}

export function bestPopularityTier(current: unknown, next: unknown): "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" {
  const left = publicPopularityTier(current);
  const right = publicPopularityTier(next);
  return POPULARITY_TIER_RANK[right] > POPULARITY_TIER_RANK[left] ? right : left;
}

export function popularityFlameMarkup(tier: unknown): string {
  const safeTier = publicPopularityTier(tier);
  if (safeTier === "standard") return "";
  return `<style>${POPULARITY_MARKER_CSS}</style>${FLAME_SVG}`;
}

export function wrapMarkerWithPopularityTier(innerHtml: string, tier: "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" | null | undefined): string {
  const safeTier = publicPopularityTier(tier);
  return `<style>${POPULARITY_MARKER_CSS}</style><div class="tz-popularity-marker" data-popularity-tier="${safeTier}">${innerHtml}${safeTier === "standard" ? "" : FLAME_SVG}</div>`;
}

export function syncPopularityMarkerInteraction(
  element: { setAttribute(name: string, value: string): void; removeAttribute(name: string): void },
  interacting: boolean,
): void {
  if (interacting) element.setAttribute("data-map-interacting", "true");
  else element.removeAttribute("data-map-interacting");
}

===== apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/published-popularity.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 5687 | sha256 84ee9979ace9756456178449f6d831fe796cee0d68d66e155fc127aaef4163f1 =====
import publication from './public-popularity-publication.json';
import type { Restaurant } from '@/types/restaurant';

export type PublishedPopularityTier = 'fire' | 'ember' | 'rainbow' | 'standard';
export type PublishedPopularityLookup = (restaurantId: string) => PublishedPopularityTier;
export interface PublicPopularityPublication {
    readonly schemaVersion: 2;
    readonly publicationVersion: string;
    readonly sourceRevision: string | null;
    readonly entries: readonly Readonly<{ restaurantId: string; memberRestaurantIds: readonly string[]; tier: PublishedPopularityTier }>[];
}

const MAX_PUBLICATIONS = 5000;
const MAX_PUBLICATION_BYTES = 512 * 1024;
const fail = (): never => { throw new Error('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID'); };

function exactKeys(value: Record<string, unknown>, keys: readonly string[]): boolean {
    const actual = Object.keys(value);
    return actual.length === keys.length && keys.every((key) => Object.hasOwn(value, key));
}

// Shape validation is not an operator approval receipt. The source is deliberately
// empty until an operator-reviewed cohort is published through protected Git.
// Never populate it automatically from the private metrics or shadow tables.
export function parsePublicPopularityPublication(value: unknown): PublicPopularityPublication {
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return fail();
    const data = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (!exactKeys(data, ['schemaVersion', 'publicationVersion', 'sourceRevision', 'entries'])
        || data.schemaVersion !== 2
        || typeof data.publicationVersion !== 'string'
        || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$/.test(data.publicationVersion)
        || (data.sourceRevision !== null && (typeof data.sourceRevision !== 'string' || !/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/.test(data.sourceRevision)))
        || !Array.isArray(data.entries) || data.entries.length > MAX_PUBLICATIONS) return fail();
    if (data.entries.length > 0 && (data.publicationVersion === 'unpublished' || data.sourceRevision === null)) return fail();
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const entries = data.entries.map((entry: unknown) => {
        if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(entry)) return fail();
        const row = entry as Record<string, unknown>;
        if (!exactKeys(row, ['restaurantId', 'memberRestaurantIds', 'tier'])
            || typeof row.restaurantId !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(row.restaurantId)
            || typeof row.tier !== 'string' || !['fire', 'ember', 'rainbow', 'standard'].includes(row.tier)
            || !Array.isArray(row.memberRestaurantIds) || !row.memberRestaurantIds.length
            || row.memberRestaurantIds.length > MAX_PUBLICATIONS) return fail();
        const members: string[] = [];
        for (const member of row.memberRestaurantIds) {
            if (typeof member !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(member)
                || seen.has(member) || (members.length > 0 && members[members.length - 1] >= member)
                || seen.size >= MAX_PUBLICATIONS) return fail();
            members.push(member);
            seen.add(member);
        }
        // Explicit reviewed membership binds the grade, not whichever video row
        // the compact/full projection selects as its display representative.
        if (members[0] !== row.restaurantId) return fail();
        return Object.freeze({ restaurantId: row.restaurantId,
            memberRestaurantIds: Object.freeze(members), tier: row.tier as PublishedPopularityTier });
    });
    const result = { schemaVersion: 2 as const, publicationVersion: data.publicationVersion,
        sourceRevision: data.sourceRevision as string | null, entries: Object.freeze(entries) };
    if (new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(result)).byteLength > MAX_PUBLICATION_BYTES) return fail();
    return Object.freeze(result);
}

function lookupFor(publication: PublicPopularityPublication): PublishedPopularityLookup {
    const tiers = new Map<string, PublishedPopularityTier>();
    for (const entry of publication.entries) {
        for (const member of entry.memberRestaurantIds) tiers.set(member, entry.tier);
    }
    return (restaurantId) => tiers.get(restaurantId) ?? 'standard';
}

export function createPublishedPopularityLookup(value: unknown): PublishedPopularityLookup {
    return lookupFor(parsePublicPopularityPublication(value));
}

export const publicPopularityPublication = parsePublicPopularityPublication(publication);
export const lookupPublishedPopularityTier = lookupFor(publicPopularityPublication);

export function resolvePublishedPopularityTier(
    restaurant: { id: string; mergedRestaurants?: readonly { id: string }[] | null },
    lookup: PublishedPopularityLookup = lookupPublishedPopularityTier,
    members = restaurant.mergedRestaurants,
): PublishedPopularityTier {
    if (lookup === lookupPublishedPopularityTier && publicPopularityPublication.entries.length === 0) return 'standard';
    const tier = lookup(restaurant.id);
    // A new/unpublished member or conflicting publication invalidates the group
    // display. Never choose the highest grade or trust the display representative.
    for (const member of members ?? []) {
        if (lookup(member.id) !== tier) return 'standard';
    }
    return tier;
}

export function projectPublishedPopularityTier(
    restaurant: Restaurant,
    lookup: PublishedPopularityLookup = lookupPublishedPopularityTier,
): Restaurant {
    const tier = resolvePublishedPopularityTier(restaurant, lookup);
    return restaurant.popularity_tier === tier ? restaurant : { ...restaurant, popularity_tier: tier };
}


===== apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/records.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 4163 | sha256 b9c6b9c08e8044cf839b46d2b5295199cc6f644ea4f51c0169f4b731022a7883 =====
import { EvidenceContractCodes, EvidenceContractError } from "./errors";
import {
  isPopularityTier,
  requireCanonicalKey,
  requireIsoTimestamp,
  requireVersionToken,
  safeCount,
} from "./guards";
import type {
  FetchDecision,
  FetchRetryBudget,
  PopularityMetricRecord,
  PopularityTier,
  PopularityTierAssignment,
  ScoringCohortIdentity,
} from "./types";

export function classifyFetch(status: number, challenge: boolean): FetchDecision {
  if (challenge || status === 401 || status === 403) return "stop_source";
  if (status === 429 || (Number.isInteger(status) && status >= 500 && status <= 599)) return "retry_later";
  if (Number.isInteger(status) && status >= 200 && status < 300) return "accept";
  return "stop_source";
}

export function mayRetryFetch(decision: FetchDecision, budget: FetchRetryBudget): boolean {
  if (decision !== "retry_later") return false;
  if (budget.circuitOpen) return false;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(budget.attemptsUsed) || budget.attemptsUsed < 0) return false;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(budget.maxAttempts) || budget.maxAttempts < 0) return false;
  if (budget.attemptsUsed >= budget.maxAttempts) return false;
  if (budget.retryAfterSeconds !== null) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(budget.retryAfterSeconds) || budget.retryAfterSeconds < 0) return false;
  }
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(budget.dailyRemaining) || budget.dailyRemaining < 1) return false;
  return true;
}

export function parseTierAssignment(input: PopularityTierAssignment): PopularityTierAssignment {
  if (!isPopularityTier(input.popularity_tier)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidPopularityTier);
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(input.popularity_score)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidPopularityScore);
  }
  return {
    scoring_version: requireVersionToken(input.scoring_version, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidScoringVersion),
    cohort_version: requireVersionToken(input.cohort_version, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCohortVersion),
    computed_at: requireIsoTimestamp(input.computed_at),
    popularity_tier: input.popularity_tier,
    popularity_score: input.popularity_score,
  };
}

export function assertSameScoringCohort(
  assignments: readonly PopularityTierAssignment[],
): ScoringCohortIdentity {
  const parsedAssignments = assignments.map(parseTierAssignment);
  const first = parsedAssignments[0];
  if (!first) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.emptyCohort);
  }
  for (const parsed of parsedAssignments.slice(1)) {
    if (
      parsed.scoring_version !== first.scoring_version ||
      parsed.cohort_version !== first.cohort_version ||
      parsed.computed_at !== first.computed_at
    ) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.mixedScoringCohort);
    }
  }
  return {
    scoring_version: first.scoring_version,
    cohort_version: first.cohort_version,
    computed_at: first.computed_at,
  };
}

export function mergePopularityTierRecords(
  assignments: readonly PopularityTierAssignment[],
): {
  cohort: ScoringCohortIdentity | null;
  tiers: PopularityTier[];
  picksHighestTier: false;
} {
  if (assignments.length === 0) {
    return { cohort: null, tiers: [], picksHighestTier: false };
  }
  const parsed = assignments.map(parseTierAssignment);
  return {
    cohort: assertSameScoringCohort(parsed),
    tiers: parsed.map((item) => item.popularity_tier),
    picksHighestTier: false,
  };
}

export function parsePopularityMetric(input: PopularityMetricRecord): PopularityMetricRecord {
  const assignment = parseTierAssignment(input);
  const sourceRevision = safeCount(input.source_revision, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
  if (sourceRevision === null) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidSourceRevision);
  }
  return {
    canonical_key: requireCanonicalKey(input.canonical_key),
    source_revision: sourceRevision,
    scoring_version: assignment.scoring_version,
    cohort_version: assignment.cohort_version,
    popularity_score: assignment.popularity_score,
    popularity_tier: assignment.popularity_tier,
    computed_at: assignment.computed_at,
  };
}

    return `${videoId}/${timestampMs}_${contentSha256.slice(0, STORAGE_HASH_PREFIX_LENGTH)}.webp`;
}

===== apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/upload-evidence-frame.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 2259 | sha256 d1ce82e82f186752229832b21adc33298e07626a76f6661b883fa2e1125e48e7 =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";

import { buildStorageObjectKey } from "./frames";

const WEBP_KEY_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}\/\d+_[a-f0-9]{12}\.webp$/;

export interface EvidenceObjectStore {
  exists(key: string): Promise<boolean>;
  put(key: string, bytes: Uint8Array, contentType: "image/webp"): Promise<{ publicUrl: string | null }>;
}

export class EvidenceUploadError extends Error {
  readonly code: string;

  constructor(code: string) {
    super(code);
    this.name = "EvidenceUploadError";
    this.code = code;
  }
}

export function assertWebpBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): void {
  if (!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array) || bytes.byteLength < 12) {
    throw new EvidenceUploadError("invalid_webp");
  }
  const riff = String.fromCharCode(bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]);
  const webp = String.fromCharCode(bytes[8], bytes[9], bytes[10], bytes[11]);
  if (riff !== "RIFF" || webp !== "WEBP") {
    throw new EvidenceUploadError("invalid_webp");
  }
}

export function uploadObjectKey(videoId: string, timestampMs: number, contentSha256: string): string {
  const key = buildStorageObjectKey(videoId, timestampMs, contentSha256);
  if (!WEBP_KEY_PATTERN.test(key) || key.endsWith(`/${timestampMs}.webp`)) {
    throw new EvidenceUploadError("invalid_storage_key");
  }
  return key;
}

export async function uploadEvidenceWebp(
  input: {
    videoId: string;
    timestampMs: number;
    contentSha256: string;
    bytes: Uint8Array;
  },
  store: EvidenceObjectStore,
): Promise<{ storageObjectKey: string; imageUrl: string | null }> {
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    throw new EvidenceUploadError("storage_client_forbidden_in_browser");
  }
  assertWebpBytes(input.bytes);
  const actualHash = createHash("sha256").update(input.bytes).digest("hex");
  if (actualHash !== input.contentSha256) {
    throw new EvidenceUploadError("content_hash_mismatch");
  }
  const storageObjectKey = uploadObjectKey(input.videoId, input.timestampMs, input.contentSha256);
  if (await store.exists(storageObjectKey)) {
    throw new EvidenceUploadError("storage_key_collision");
  }
  const stored = await store.put(storageObjectKey, input.bytes, "image/webp");
  return {
    storageObjectKey,
    imageUrl: stored.publicUrl,
  };
}

import type { Restaurant, YoutubeMeta } from '@/types/restaurant';
import { extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId } from '@/lib/youtube-url';

export type HomeMapThemeFilterId =
    | 'hot-view'
    | 'comment-hot'
    | 'fresh-video'
    | 'repeat-video'
    | 'fan-signal';

export type HomeMapThemeFilter = {
    id: HomeMapThemeFilterId;
    label: string;
    ariaLabel: string;
    description: string;
};

const FRESH_VIDEO_DAYS = 90;
const REPEAT_VIDEO_MIN_COUNT = 2;
const TOP_BAND_RATIO = 0.2;
const YOUTUBE_METADATA_BACKED_HOME_MAP_THEME_FILTER_IDS = [
    'hot-view',
    'comment-hot',
    'fresh-video',
    'fan-signal',
] as const satisfies ReadonlyArray<HomeMapThemeFilterId>;

    },

    },
