===== apps/web/scripts/build-public-popularity.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 4439 | sha256 a6f71ce2fea76bc1183b09961cc153d97bf8d9970f87fed1ae49414893212dcd =====
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { mergeRestaurants } from '../hooks/use-restaurants';
import { extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId } from '../lib/youtube-url';
import { parsePublicPopularityPublication } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/published-popularity';
import { restaurantMatchesOverseasCountry } from '../lib/overseas-region-matching';
import { OVERSEAS_REGIONS } from '../constants/overseas-regions';
import type { Tables } from '../integrations/supabase/types';

export async function buildPublicPopularity(sourcePath: string, metricsPath: string, revision: string) {
  const sourceBytes = await readFile(sourcePath);
  const metricBytes = await readFile(metricsPath);
  const source = JSON.parse(sourceBytes.toString()) as { rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> };
  const metrics = JSON.parse(metricBytes.toString()) as { rows: Array<{ video_id: string; view_count: number | string; fetched_at: string; bucket_started_at: string }> };
  const byVideo = new Map(metrics.rows.map((r) => [r.video_id, r]));
  const rows = source.rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, approved_name: r.name })) as unknown as Tables<'restaurants'>[];
  const countries = [...new Set(Object.values(OVERSEAS_REGIONS).map((r) => r.country))];
  const groups = mergeRestaurants(rows, () => 'standard').map((r) => {
    const members = r.mergedRestaurants?.length ? r.mergedRestaurants : [r];
    const ids = [...new Set(members.map((m) => m.id))].sort();
    const videos = [...new Set(members.map((m) => extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(m.youtube_link ?? '')).filter((v): v is string => !!v))];
    const observed = videos.flatMap((id) => {
      const metric = byVideo.get(id); const count = Number(metric?.view_count);
      return metric && Number.isSafeInteger(count) && count > 0 ? [{ videoId: id, count, observedAt: metric.fetched_at || metric.bucket_started_at }] : [];
    });
    const overseas = countries.some((country) => restaurantMatchesOverseasCountry(r, country)) || Number(r.lat) < 32 || Number(r.lat) > 40 || Number(r.lng) < 124 || Number(r.lng) > 132;
    return { restaurantId: ids[0], memberRestaurantIds: ids, name: r.name, cohort: overseas ? 'overseas' : 'domestic', viewCount: observed.length ? Math.max(...observed.map((m) => m.count)) : null, observed };
  });
  const thresholds = ['domestic', 'overseas'].map((cohort) => {
    const counts = groups.filter((g) => g.cohort === cohort && g.viewCount !== null).map((g) => g.viewCount!).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const percentile = (p: number) => counts.length ? counts[Math.max(0, Math.ceil(counts.length * p) - 1)] : null;
    return { cohort, knownGroups: counts.length, fireAt: percentile(0.98), emberAt: percentile(0.95), rainbowAt: percentile(0.85) };
  });
  const entries = groups.map((group) => {
    const cut = thresholds.find((c) => c.cohort === group.cohort)!;
    const tier = group.viewCount === null || cut.fireAt === null ? 'standard' : group.viewCount >= cut.fireAt ? 'fire' : group.viewCount >= cut.emberAt! ? 'ember' : group.viewCount >= cut.rainbowAt! ? 'rainbow' : 'standard';
    return { restaurantId: group.restaurantId, memberRestaurantIds: group.memberRestaurantIds, tier };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.restaurantId.localeCompare(b.restaurantId));
  const publication = parsePublicPopularityPublication({ schemaVersion: 2, publicationVersion: 'video-views-20261022-v1', sourceRevision: revision, entries });
  const sha = (b: Buffer) => createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
  return { publication, evidence: { policy: 'map-cohort-max-linked-video-views-nearest-rank-p95-p85-v1', definition: 'Most-viewed linked video per merged restaurant, ranked separately within domestic and overseas approved restaurant cohorts. This measures video reach, not food quality.', sourceSha256: sha(sourceBytes), metricsSha256: sha(metricBytes), thresholds, groups } };
}

if (import.meta.main) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const result = await buildPublicPopularity(args[0], args[1], args[2]);
  await writeFile(args[3], JSON.stringify(result.publication, null, 2) + '\n');
  await writeFile(args[4], JSON.stringify(result.evidence, null, 2) + '\n');
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ groups: result.publication.entries.length, tiers: result.publication.entries.reduce((a, e) => ({ ...a, [e.tier]: (a[e.tier] ?? 0) + 1 }), {} as Record<string, number>), thresholds: result.evidence.thresholds }));
}


===== apps/web/scripts/publish-evidence-frames.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 5449 | sha256 296ac93773968dda8b5daa8a8166ff234e782acbc31f8a9b178910221babee99 =====
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { lstat, mkdir, readFile, realpath, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { parseEvidenceFrame } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/frames';
import { uploadEvidenceWebp, type EvidenceObjectStore } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/upload-evidence-frame';
import { extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId } from '../lib/youtube-url';

type ReviewedFrame = { restaurant_id: string; source_file: string; frame: unknown };

export async function previewFramePublication(manifestPath: string, sourceDirectory: string) {
  const source = await realpath(sourceDirectory);
  const manifest = JSON.parse(await readFile(manifestPath, 'utf8')) as { restaurantSourceSha256: string; frames?: ReviewedFrame[] };
  const restaurantBytes = await readFile(resolve(dirname(manifestPath), 'approved-source-rows.json'));
  if (createHash('sha256').update(restaurantBytes).digest('hex') !== manifest.restaurantSourceSha256) throw new Error('restaurant_source_hash_mismatch');
  const restaurants = JSON.parse(restaurantBytes.toString()) as { rows: Array<{ id: string; status: string; youtube_link: string }> };
  const byRestaurant = new Map(restaurants.rows.map((row) => [row.id, row]));
  if (!Array.isArray(manifest.frames) || manifest.frames.length < 1 || manifest.frames.length > 5000) throw new Error('invalid_frame_manifest');
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const seenKeys = new Set<string>();
  const entries = [];
  for (const item of manifest.frames) {
    if (!/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/.test(item.restaurant_id) || typeof item.source_file !== 'string') throw new Error('invalid_frame_owner');
    const frame = parseEvidenceFrame(item.frame);
    const restaurant = byRestaurant.get(item.restaurant_id);
    if (!restaurant || restaurant.status !== 'approved' || extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(restaurant.youtube_link) !== frame.video_id) throw new Error('frame_restaurant_video_mismatch');
    if (frame.moderation_status !== 'approved' || frame.evidence_level !== 'SOURCE_OBSERVED') throw new Error('frame_not_reviewed');
    if (seen.has(frame.frame_id) || seenKeys.has(frame.storage_object_key)) throw new Error('duplicate_frame');
    seen.add(frame.frame_id); seenKeys.add(frame.storage_object_key);
    const file = await realpath(resolve(source, item.source_file));
    if (!file.startsWith(source + sep)) throw new Error('source_path_outside_root');
    const bytes = await readFile(file);
    // Run exactly the production upload validation during preview too.
    await uploadEvidenceWebp({ videoId: frame.video_id, timestampMs: frame.timestamp_ms, contentSha256: frame.content_sha256, bytes }, { exists: async () => false, put: async () => ({ publicUrl: null }) });
    entries.push({ restaurant_id: item.restaurant_id, frame, file, bytes });
  }
  const digest = createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(entries.map(({ restaurant_id, frame }) => ({ restaurant_id, frame })))).digest('hex');
  return { entries, previewHash: digest };
}

export async function publishFrameFiles(manifestPath: string, sourceDirectory: string, outputDirectory: string, confirmationHash: string) {
  const preview = await previewFramePublication(manifestPath, sourceDirectory);
  if (confirmationHash !== preview.previewHash) throw new Error('publication_confirmation_mismatch');
  const output = resolve(outputDirectory);
  await mkdir(output, { recursive: true });
  const physicalOutput = await realpath(output);
  for (const { frame, bytes } of preview.entries) {
    const file = resolve(output, frame.storage_object_key);
    if (!file.startsWith(output + sep)) throw new Error('output_path_outside_root');
    await mkdir(dirname(file), { recursive: true });
    if (!(await realpath(dirname(file))).startsWith(physicalOutput + sep)) throw new Error('output_symlink_outside_root');
    let existing: Buffer | null = null;
    try {
      if ((await lstat(file)).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error('output_file_is_symlink');
      existing = await readFile(file);
    } catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
    if (existing) {
      if (!existing.equals(bytes)) throw new Error('immutable_frame_collision');
      continue;
    }
    const store: EvidenceObjectStore = {
      exists: async () => false,
      put: async (_key, body) => { await writeFile(file, body, { flag: 'wx' }); return { publicUrl: null }; },
    };
    await uploadEvidenceWebp({ videoId: frame.video_id, timestampMs: frame.timestamp_ms, contentSha256: frame.content_sha256, bytes }, store);
  }
  return { previewHash: preview.previewHash, frames: preview.entries.map(({ restaurant_id, frame }) => ({ restaurant_id, ...frame })) };
}

if (import.meta.main) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const arg = (key: string) => args[args.indexOf(key) + 1];
  if (!args.includes('--manifest') || !args.includes('--source-dir')) throw new Error('manifest_and_source_required');
  const result = args.includes('--apply')
    ? await publishFrameFiles(arg('--manifest'), arg('--source-dir'), args.includes('--output-dir') ? arg('--output-dir') : 'public/evidence-frames', arg('--confirm'))
    : await previewFramePublication(arg('--manifest'), arg('--source-dir'));
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ previewHash: result.previewHash, frames: 'frames' in result ? result.frames.length : result.entries.length, applied: args.includes('--apply') }));
}

function webpBytes(): Uint8Array {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(12);
  bytes.set([0x52, 0x49, 0x46, 0x46, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x57, 0x45, 0x42, 0x50]);
  return bytes;
}

function fakeFrameClient(
  data: Array<{ moderation_status: string }> | null,
  error: { code?: string } | null = null,
): EvidenceFrameModerationClient {
  return {
    from(table) {
      if (table !== "restaurant_evidence_frames") throw new Error("wrong_table");
      return {
        update() {
          return {
            eq() {
              return {
                eq() {
                  return {
                    select: async () => ({ data, error }),
                  };
                },
              };
            },
          };
        },
      };
    },
  };
}

function frame(overrides: Parameters<typeof buildEvidenceFrame>[0] extends infer T ? Partial<T> : never = {}) {
  return buildEvidenceFrame({
    frame_id: "frame-sign-1",
    content_sha256: SHA,
    video_id: VIDEO_ID,
    timestamp_ms: 125500,
    frame_type: "storefront_sign",
    evidence_level: "SOURCE_OBSERVED",
    confidence_level: "certain",
    moderation_status: "approved",
    detected_shop_name: "회전초밥",
    detected_branch_name: null,
    image_url: null,
    ...overrides,
  });
}

  test("uploads only the immutable webp key and refuses collisions", async () => {
    const bytes = webpBytes();

    const stored = new Map<string, Uint8Array>();
    const result = await uploadEvidenceWebp(
      { videoId: VIDEO_ID, timestampMs: 125500, contentSha256, bytes },
      {
        exists: async (key) => stored.has(key),
        put: async (key, body) => {
          stored.set(key, body);
          return { publicUrl: `https://cdn.example/${key}` };
        },
      },
    );
    expect(result.storageObjectKey).toBe(`${VIDEO_ID}/125500_${contentSha256.slice(0, 12)}.webp`);

      },
    )).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "storage_key_collision" });

    expect(positions).toEqual([...positions].sort((left, right) => left - right));
  });

  test("render summary records percentiles without a 60fps gate", () => {

import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, rm, symlink, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { buildEvidenceFrame } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/frames';
import { previewFramePublication, publishFrameFiles } from '../scripts/publish-evidence-frames';
import { buildPublicPopularity } from '../scripts/build-public-popularity';
import publication from '../lib/evidence-engineering/public-popularity-publication.json';
import { wrapMarkerWithPopularityTier } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/popularity-marker';
import { buildOverseasMarkerHtml } from '../lib/overseas-map-marker-helpers';

const cleanups: string[] = [];
afterEach(async () => { for (const dir of cleanups.splice(0)) await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); });
const sha = (value: Buffer) => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');

async function fixture() {
  const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'tzudong-publication-test-')); cleanups.push(root);
  const source = join(root, 'source'); const output = join(root, 'public'); await mkdir(source);
  const reviewed = JSON.parse(await readFile(resolve(import.meta.dir, '../../../docs/evidence/marker-evidence-live-20261021-v1/reviewed-frames.json'), 'utf8'));
  const first = reviewed.frames[0];
  const bytes = await readFile(resolve(import.meta.dir, '../public/evidence-frames', first.frame.storage_object_key));
  await writeFile(join(source, 'frame.webp'), bytes);
  const restaurantId = 'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa';
  const frame = buildEvidenceFrame({ ...first.frame, frame_id: 'test-frame' });
  const restaurantBytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ rows: [{ id: restaurantId, status: 'approved', youtube_link: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${frame.video_id}` }] }));
  await writeFile(join(root, 'approved-source-rows.json'), restaurantBytes);
  const manifest = { restaurantSourceSha256: sha(restaurantBytes), frames: [{ restaurant_id: restaurantId, source_file: 'frame.webp', frame: { ...frame } }] };
  const path = join(root, 'manifest.json'); await writeFile(path, JSON.stringify(manifest));
  return { root, source, output, bytes, frame, manifest, path };
}

describe('reviewed evidence publication', () => {
  test('publishes hash-verified WebP idempotently and refuses a wrong confirmation or immutable collision', async () => {
    const f = await fixture(); const preview = await previewFramePublication(f.path, f.source);
    await expect(publishFrameFiles(f.path, f.source, f.output, 'wrong')).rejects.toThrow('publication_confirmation_mismatch');
    await publishFrameFiles(f.path, f.source, f.output, preview.previewHash);
    const target = join(f.output, f.frame.storage_object_key);
    expect(await readFile(target)).toEqual(f.bytes);
    await publishFrameFiles(f.path, f.source, f.output, preview.previewHash);
    await writeFile(target, 'collision');
    await expect(publishFrameFiles(f.path, f.source, f.output, preview.previewHash)).rejects.toThrow('immutable_frame_collision');
  });

  test('rejects unreviewed frames, altered bytes, source traversal and mismatched restaurant videos', async () => {
    const f = await fixture();
    f.manifest.frames[0].frame.moderation_status = 'pending'; await writeFile(f.path, JSON.stringify(f.manifest));
    await expect(previewFramePublication(f.path, f.source)).rejects.toThrow('frame_not_reviewed');
    f.manifest.frames[0].frame.moderation_status = 'approved';
    f.manifest.frames[0].frame.video_id = 'abc123DEF45';
    f.manifest.frames[0].frame = buildEvidenceFrame(f.manifest.frames[0].frame); await writeFile(f.path, JSON.stringify(f.manifest));
    await expect(previewFramePublication(f.path, f.source)).rejects.toThrow('frame_restaurant_video_mismatch');
    f.manifest.frames[0].frame = f.frame; f.manifest.frames[0].source_file = '../outside.webp';
    await writeFile(join(f.root, 'outside.webp'), f.bytes); await writeFile(f.path, JSON.stringify(f.manifest));
    await expect(previewFramePublication(f.path, f.source)).rejects.toThrow('source_path_outside_root');
    f.manifest.frames[0].source_file = 'frame.webp'; await writeFile(f.path, JSON.stringify(f.manifest)); await writeFile(join(f.source, 'frame.webp'), Buffer.concat([f.bytes, Buffer.from([1])]));
    await expect(previewFramePublication(f.path, f.source)).rejects.toThrow('content_hash_mismatch');
  });

  test('refuses publication through an output symlink', async () => {
    const f = await fixture(); const preview = await previewFramePublication(f.path, f.source);
    const outside = join(f.root, 'outside'); await mkdir(outside); await mkdir(f.output);
    await symlink(outside, dirname(join(f.output, f.frame.storage_object_key)), 'dir');
    await expect(publishFrameFiles(f.path, f.source, f.output, preview.previewHash)).rejects.toThrow('output_symlink_outside_root');
  });
});

    const overseas = buildOverseasMarkerHtml({ imagePath: '/images/maker-images/webp/asian.webp', name: 'actual cohort marker', popularityTier: fire.tier });
    for (const html of [domestic, overseas]) {
      expect(html.match(/class="tz-popularity-flame"/g)).toHaveLength(1);

      expect(html).not.toContain('data-flame-count');

  });

import { getNaverIndividualMarkerVisual } from '../lib/naver-map-marker-visuals';

describe('naver map marker visuals', () => {

            id: 'tier-mutation-marker',
            categories: ['한식'],
            popularity_tier: 'standard',
        };
        for (const tier of ['standard', 'fire', 'rainbow', null] as const) {
            restaurant.popularity_tier = tier;
            for (const selected of [false, true]) {
                const visual = getNaverIndividualMarkerVisual(restaurant, selected);
                expect(visual.content).toContain(`class="tz-popularity-marker" data-popularity-tier="${tier ?? 'standard'}"`);
                expect(getNaverIndividualMarkerVisual(restaurant, selected)).toBe(visual);
            }
        }
    });

    test('keeps shared visual cache entries distinct across refreshed rows and overlay markers', () => {
        const restaurant = { id: 'tier-refetch-marker', categories: ['분식'] };
        for (const overlay of [[], ['trend']] as const) {
            for (const tier of ['fire', 'rainbow', 'standard', null] as const) {
                for (const selected of [false, true]) {
                    const visual = getNaverIndividualMarkerVisual({ ...restaurant, popularity_tier: tier }, selected, overlay);
                    expect(visual.content).toContain(`class="tz-popularity-marker" data-popularity-tier="${tier ?? 'standard'}"`);
                    expect(visual.content).toContain(`data-restaurant-marker-kind="${overlay.length ? 'trend' : 'category'}"`);
                }
            }
        }
    });

        expect(getNaverIndividualMarkerVisual(restaurant, false)).toBe(first);

    });

    test('returns selected marker payload', () => {

        expect(visual.anchor).toEqual({ x: 18, y: 18 });

        expect(visual.content).toContain('data-restaurant-marker-kind="category"');
        expect(visual.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/webp/korean.webp');
        expect(visual.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/korean.png');
        expect(visual.content).toContain('type="image/webp"');
    });

    test('returns unselected marker payload', () => {
        const visual = getNaverIndividualMarkerVisual({ categories: [], category: ['분식'] }, false);
        expect(visual.anchor).toEqual({ x: 14, y: 14 });
        expect(visual.zIndex).toBe(1);

        expect(visual.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/snack_bar.png');
        expect(visual.content).toContain('type="image/webp"');

import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';

import { MARKER_IMAGE_FALLBACK } from '../lib/html-escape';
import {
    applyOverseasMarkerSelectedState,
    buildOverseasMarkerHtml,
    getOverseasMarkerActiveId,
} from '../lib/overseas-map-marker-helpers';

describe('overseas map marker helpers', () => {
    test('builds class-only marker HTML with valid local and HTTPS images', () => {
        for (const imagePath of [
            '/images/maker-images/asian.png',
            'https://cdn.example.com/markers/asian.png',
        ]) {
            const html = buildOverseasMarkerHtml({
                imagePath,
                name: '테스트 식당',
            });

            expect(html).toContain(`src="${imagePath}"`);
            expect(html).toContain('alt="테스트 식당"');
            expect(html).toContain('class="marker-container relative h-full w-full cursor-pointer hover:scale-110"');
            expect(html).toContain('class="h-full w-full object-contain"');
            expect(html).toContain('draggable="false"');
            expect(html).not.toContain('style=');
        }
    });

    test('fails closed for unsafe marker image values and escapes alt text', () => {
        const unsafeImagePaths = [
            'javascript:alert(1)',
            'data:image/svg+xml,<svg onload=alert(1)>',
            '//attacker.example/marker.png',
            'https://user:password@cdn.example.com/marker.png',
            '/images/%2e%2e/private.png',
            '/images/maker-images/asian.png" onerror="alert(1)',
        ];

        for (const imagePath of unsafeImagePaths) {
            const html = buildOverseasMarkerHtml({
                imagePath,
                name: '해외 "식당" <img>',
            });

            expect(html).toContain(`src="${MARKER_IMAGE_FALLBACK}"`);
            expect(html).toContain('alt="해외 &quot;식당&quot; &lt;img&gt;"');
            expect(html).not.toContain('onerror="alert(1)');
            expect(html).not.toContain('<img>');
        }
    });

    test('prefers selected restaurant id over searched id', () => {
        expect(getOverseasMarkerActiveId({
            searchedRestaurantId: 'searched',
            selectedRestaurantId: 'selected',
        })).toBe('selected');
        expect(getOverseasMarkerActiveId({
            searchedRestaurantId: 'searched',
            selectedRestaurantId: null,
        })).toBe('searched');
    });

    test('toggles only fixed size and selected classes without style mutation', () => {
        const markerClassNames = new Set<string>();
        const containerClassNames = new Set<string>();
        const markerElement = {
            classList: {
                add: (...names: string[]) => names.forEach((name) => markerClassNames.add(name)),
                remove: (...names: string[]) => names.forEach((name) => markerClassNames.delete(name)),
            },
        } as unknown as HTMLElement;
        const container = {
            classList: {
                add: (...names: string[]) => names.forEach((name) => containerClassNames.add(name)),
                remove: (...names: string[]) => names.forEach((name) => containerClassNames.delete(name)),
            },
        } as unknown as HTMLElement;

        applyOverseasMarkerSelectedState({
            container,
            isSelected: true,
            markerElement,
        });
        expect([...markerClassNames].sort()).toEqual(['!h-[42px]', '!w-[42px]', 'selected']);
        expect([...containerClassNames].sort()).toEqual(['scale-110']);
        expect('style' in markerElement).toBe(false);
        expect('style' in container).toBe(false);

        applyOverseasMarkerSelectedState({
            container,
            isSelected: false,
            markerElement,
        });
        expect([...markerClassNames].sort()).toEqual(['!h-8', '!w-8']);
        expect([...containerClassNames].sort()).toEqual(['scale-100']);
        expect('style' in markerElement).toBe(false);
        expect('style' in container).toBe(false);
    });
});


===== apps/web/tests-unit/public-popularity-publication.test.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 12531 | sha256 6c552c1224825bb577e2d862acb4be325426064793e616e3981f19157c3933bb =====
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { GET } from '../app/api/public-popularity/route';
import { mergeRestaurants } from '../hooks/use-restaurants';
import { buildRestaurantDetailFromMergeRows } from '../hooks/use-restaurant-detail';
import {
    createPublishedPopularityLookup, parsePublicPopularityPublication,
    projectPublishedPopularityTier, publicPopularityPublication, resolvePublishedPopularityTier,
} from '../lib/evidence-engineering/published-popularity';
import { buildRestaurantMarkerKindSignature } from '../lib/restaurant-marker-kind';
import { buildMarkerRenderSignature, shouldSkipMarkerUpdate } from '../lib/map-render-guard';
import type { Tables } from '../integrations/supabase/types';
import type { Restaurant } from '../types/restaurant';

const snapshot = () => ({ schemaVersion: 2, publicationVersion: 'test-cohort-1',
    sourceRevision: 'a'.repeat(40), entries: [{ restaurantId: 'public-a', memberRestaurantIds: ['public-a'], tier: 'fire' }] });
const dbRow = (id: string, extra = {}) => ({ id, approved_name: '검증 식당', status: 'approved',
    road_address: '공개 주소', lat: 37.5, lng: 127, categories: ['한식'],
    created_at: '2026-10-17T00:00:00Z', ...extra } as unknown as Tables<'restaurants'>);

describe('operator-published public popularity', () => {
    test('rejects private fields, duplicate IDs, invalid grades and unreviewed nonempty cohorts', () => {
        const valid = snapshot();
        const invalid = [
            { ...valid, metrics: { score: 100 } },
            { ...valid, entries: [{ ...valid.entries[0], raw_score: 100 }] },
            { ...valid, entries: [...valid.entries, ...valid.entries] },
            { ...valid, entries: [{ ...valid.entries[0], tier: 'private-shadow' }] },
            { ...valid, entries: [{ ...valid.entries[0], tier: { toString() { throw new Error('coerced'); } } }] },
            { ...valid, publicationVersion: 'unpublished' },
            { ...valid, schemaVersion: 1 },
            { ...valid, sourceRevision: null },
        ];
        for (const value of invalid) expect(() => parsePublicPopularityPublication(value)).toThrow('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID');
    });

    test('bounds entry count and serialized bytes and freezes independent copies', () => {
        const valid = snapshot();
        const parsed = parsePublicPopularityPublication(valid);
        valid.entries[0].tier = 'rainbow';
        valid.entries[0].memberRestaurantIds.push('public-z');
        expect(parsed.entries[0].tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(parsed.entries[0].memberRestaurantIds).toEqual(['public-a']);
        expect(Object.isFrozen(parsed.entries[0])).toBe(true);
        expect(Object.isFrozen(parsed.entries)).toBe(true);
        expect(() => parsePublicPopularityPublication({ ...valid,
            entries: Array.from({ length: 5001 }, (_, i) => ({ restaurantId: `r-${i}`, tier: 'fire' })),
        })).toThrow('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID');
        expect(() => parsePublicPopularityPublication({ ...valid,
            entries: Array.from({ length: 4000 }, (_, i) => { const id = `${i}-`.padEnd(128, 'a');
                return { restaurantId: id, memberRestaurantIds: [id], tier: 'fire' }; }),
        })).toThrow('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID');
        expect(Object.isFrozen(parsed.entries[0].memberRestaurantIds)).toBe(true);
    });

    test('list and detail derive the same tier without a database tier column; stale/raw tiers cannot promote it', () => {
        const lookup = createPublishedPopularityLookup(snapshot());
        const rows = [dbRow('public-a'), dbRow('private-b', { approved_name: '다른 식당', road_address: '다른 주소', lng: 128, popularity_tier: 'rainbow' })];
        const before = structuredClone(rows);
        const list = mergeRestaurants(rows, lookup);
        expect(list.find((r) => r.id === 'public-a')?.popularity_tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(list.find((r) => r.id === 'private-b')?.popularity_tier).toBe('standard');
        for (const item of list) {
            const stale = { ...item, popularity_tier: 'rainbow' as const };
            expect(buildRestaurantDetailFromMergeRows(stale, rows.filter((r) => r.id === item.id), lookup)?.popularity_tier)
                .toBe(item.popularity_tier);
            expect(buildRestaurantDetailFromMergeRows(stale, [], lookup)?.popularity_tier).toBe(item.popularity_tier);
        }
        expect(rows).toEqual(before);
        expect(projectPublishedPopularityTier(list[0], lookup)).toBe(list[0]);
    });

    test('merged representatives use their published identity and do not inherit a child grade', () => {
        const lookup = createPublishedPopularityLookup(snapshot());
        const rows = [dbRow('public-a', { created_at: '2026-10-16T00:00:00Z' }), dbRow('new-primary')];
        const [merged] = mergeRestaurants(rows, lookup);
        expect(merged.mergedRestaurants).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(merged.popularity_tier).toBe('standard');
        expect(buildRestaurantDetailFromMergeRows(merged, rows, lookup)?.popularity_tier).toBe('standard');
    });

    test('explicit canonical membership keeps compact, full, partial, and direct-detail grades identical', () => {
        const data = snapshot();
        data.entries[0].memberRestaurantIds.push('public-z');
        const lookup = createPublishedPopularityLookup(data);
        const rows = [dbRow('public-a', { youtube_meta: { publishedAt: '2026-01-01' } }),
            dbRow('public-z', { youtube_meta: { publishedAt: '2026-10-01' } })];
        const compact = rows.map(({ youtube_meta: _, ...row }) => row) as Tables<'restaurants'>[];
        const [mapRow] = mergeRestaurants(compact, lookup);
        const [fullRow] = mergeRestaurants(rows, lookup);
        expect(mapRow.id).toBe('public-a');
        expect(fullRow.id).toBe('public-z'); // Preserve the existing representative selection.
        expect(mapRow.popularity_tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(fullRow.popularity_tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(mergeRestaurants([rows[1]], lookup)[0].popularity_tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(buildRestaurantDetailFromMergeRows(mapRow, rows, lookup)?.popularity_tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(projectPublishedPopularityTier(rows[1] as unknown as Restaurant, lookup).popularity_tier).toBe('fire');
        expect(lookup('unpublished-new-member')).toBe('standard');
    });

    test('new members or conflicting cohorts fail closed for either display representative', () => {
        const data = snapshot();
        data.entries.push({ restaurantId: 'public-z', memberRestaurantIds: ['public-z'], tier: 'rainbow' });
        const lookup = createPublishedPopularityLookup(data);
        for (const other of ['public-z', 'unpublished-new-member']) {
            const rows = [dbRow('public-a', { youtube_meta: { publishedAt: '2026-01-01' } }),
                dbRow(other, { youtube_meta: { publishedAt: '2026-10-01' } })];
            const compact = rows.map(({ youtube_meta: _, ...row }) => row) as Tables<'restaurants'>[];
            const [mapRow] = mergeRestaurants(compact, lookup);
            const [fullRow] = mergeRestaurants(rows, lookup);
            expect(mapRow.popularity_tier).toBe('standard');
            expect(fullRow.popularity_tier).toBe('standard');
            expect(buildRestaurantDetailFromMergeRows(mapRow, rows, lookup)?.popularity_tier).toBe('standard');
            expect(resolvePublishedPopularityTier(fullRow, lookup)).toBe('standard');
        }
    });

    test('rejects ambiguous/incomplete canonical membership and bounds the whole member index', () => {
        const data = snapshot();
        for (const members of [[], ['public-z'], ['public-z', 'public-a'], ['public-a', 'public-a']]) {
            expect(() => parsePublicPopularityPublication({ ...data,
                entries: [{ ...data.entries[0], memberRestaurantIds: members }] })).toThrow('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID');
        }
        expect(() => parsePublicPopularityPublication({ ...data, entries: [data.entries[0],
            { restaurantId: 'a', memberRestaurantIds: ['a', 'public-a'], tier: 'rainbow' }] })).toThrow('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID');
        const first = Array.from({ length: 2501 }, (_, i) => `a-${String(i).padStart(4, '0')}`);
        const second = first.map((id) => id.replace('a-', 'b-'));
        const atCapacity = Array.from({ length: 5000 }, (_, i) => `r-${String(i).padStart(4, '0')}`);
        expect(createPublishedPopularityLookup({ ...data, entries: [
            { restaurantId: atCapacity[0], memberRestaurantIds: atCapacity, tier: 'fire' },
        ] })(atCapacity[4999])).toBe('fire');
        expect(() => parsePublicPopularityPublication({ ...data, entries: [
            { restaurantId: first[0], memberRestaurantIds: first, tier: 'fire' },
            { restaurantId: second[0], memberRestaurantIds: second, tier: 'rainbow' },
        ] })).toThrow('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID');
    });

    test('published membership overrides a stale caller tier and the detail panel does not mention flame counts', () => {
        const data = snapshot(); data.entries[0].memberRestaurantIds.push('public-z');
        const lookup = createPublishedPopularityLookup(data);
        expect(resolvePublishedPopularityTier({ id: 'public-a' }, lookup)).toBe('fire');
        expect(resolvePublishedPopularityTier({ id: 'public-z' }, lookup)).toBe('fire');
        expect(resolvePublishedPopularityTier({ id: 'private' }, lookup)).toBe('standard');
        const panel = readFileSync(resolve(import.meta.dir, '../components/restaurant/RestaurantDetailPanel.tsx'), 'utf8');
        expect(panel).not.toContain('PopularityMention');
        expect(panel).not.toContain('불꽃 3개');
        expect(panel).not.toContain('제일 맛있는 맛집');
    });

    test('published endpoint returns the reviewed public snapshot and stable ETag without raw scores', async () => {
        const response = GET();
        const body = await response.text();
        expect(response.status).toBe(200);
        expect(JSON.parse(body)).toEqual(publicPopularityPublication);
        expect(response.headers.get('etag')).toBe(`"${createHash('sha256').update(body).digest('hex')}"`);
        expect(response.headers.get('cache-control')).toBe('public, max-age=0, must-revalidate');
        expect(publicPopularityPublication.entries.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect(publicPopularityPublication.publicationVersion).not.toBe('unpublished');
        for (const entry of publicPopularityPublication.entries) {
            for (const id of entry.memberRestaurantIds) expect(createPublishedPopularityLookup(publicPopularityPublication)(id)).toBe(entry.tier);
        }
        expect(createPublishedPopularityLookup(publicPopularityPublication)('missing')).toBe('standard');
    });
});

describe('public tier reaches the outer Naver render guard', () => {
    const signature = (tier: unknown, retained: { selected?: unknown; searched?: unknown } = {}) => buildMarkerRenderSignature({
        zoom: 14, bounds: null, displayRestaurantIds: ['public-a'], selectedRestaurantId: 'retained', searchedRestaurantId: null,
        isClusterMode: false, isRegionalClusterMode: false, isSeoulDistrictMode: false,
        markerLayerVersion: buildRestaurantMarkerKindSignature([
            { id: 'public-a', popularity_tier: tier as Restaurant['popularity_tier'] },
        ], undefined, retained),
    });
    test('same IDs/viewport/selection refreshes fire → rainbow → standard, while normalized missing values skip', () => {
        expect(shouldSkipMarkerUpdate(signature('fire'), signature('rainbow'))).toBe(false);
        expect(shouldSkipMarkerUpdate(signature('rainbow'), signature('standard'))).toBe(false);
        expect(shouldSkipMarkerUpdate(signature(null), signature(undefined))).toBe(true);
        expect(shouldSkipMarkerUpdate(signature('unknown'), signature('standard'))).toBe(true);
    });
    test('retained selection/search grades also invalidate the unchanged-query guard', () => {
        expect(shouldSkipMarkerUpdate(signature('standard', { selected: 'fire' }), signature('standard', { selected: 'rainbow' }))).toBe(false);
        expect(shouldSkipMarkerUpdate(signature('standard', { searched: 'fire' }), signature('standard', { searched: 'standard' }))).toBe(false);
    });
});

===== apps/web/types/restaurant.ts | revision ac80e35da566db242420a62bbf3e3320b5c00e76 | dirty false | bytes 3942 | sha256 ef43ac1b93a751cb03fed1fcabecacf21d245e0b54f47a66dd67639c34ebd94b =====
import type { RestaurantDatabaseRow, ReviewDatabaseRow } from "@/integrations/supabase/types";
import type { RestaurantEvidenceFrame } from "@/lib/evidence-engineering/types";

// DB에서 가져온 기본 Restaurant 타입
type BaseRestaurant = RestaurantDatabaseRow;

// YouTube Meta 타입 정의 (DB에 저장된 실제 구조)
export interface YoutubeMeta {
    title?: string;
    ads_info?: {
        is_ads: boolean;
        what_ads: string[] | null;
    };
    duration?: number;
    is_shorts?: boolean;
    publishedAt?: string; // ISO 8601 날짜 문자열
    viewCount?: number | string | null;
    likeCount?: number | string | null;
    commentCount?: number | string | null;
}

// 호환성을 위한 확장 Restaurant 타입
export interface Restaurant extends BaseRestaurant {
    // 호환성 속성들 (기존 코드와의 호환을 위해)
    address?: string; // road_address 또는 jibun_address의 가상 속성
    category?: string[]; // categories의 별칭
    // 상세 패널 운영 주의가 읽는 선택 필드. 행에 없으면 주의 문구는 나오지 않는다.
    automaticState?: string | null;
    automatic_state?: string | null;
    reviewedStatus?: string | null;
    reviewed_status?: string | null;
    reviewed?: boolean | null;
    // 공개 행에 없으면 standard다. shadow 테이블에서는 읽지 않는다.
    popularity_tier?: "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" | null;

    // 검색 관련 (주간 인기 검색용)
    weekly_search_count: number | null; // 주간 검색 횟수

    // 마커 그룹화 시 병합된 데이터 (배열)
    mergedYoutubeLinks?: string[]; // 병합된 모든 유튜브 링크
    mergedTzuyangReviews?: string[]; // 병합된 모든 쯔양 리뷰
    mergedYoutubeMetas?: YoutubeMeta[]; // 병합된 모든 유튜브 메타
    mergedEvidenceFrames?: RestaurantEvidenceFrame[];

    // 마커 클릭 시 동일 name+jibun_address 레코드 병합 데이터
    youtube_links?: string[]; // 모든 유튜브 링크 배열
    tzuyang_reviews?: string[]; // 모든 쯔양 리뷰 배열

    // 병합된 원본 레코드들
    mergedRestaurants?: BaseRestaurant[]; // 병합된 모든 레스토랑 레코드
}

export type Review = ReviewDatabaseRow;
// categories는 배열 타입
export type RestaurantCategory = string[];

export interface RestaurantWithDetails extends Omit<Restaurant, 'visit_count'> {
    reviews?: Review[];
    visit_count?: number;
}

export interface MapMarker {
    id: string;
    position: { lat: number; lng: number };
    restaurant: Restaurant;
    markerType: "fire" | "star";
}

export interface MapBounds {
    south: number;
    west: number;
    north: number;
    east: number;
}
